  **v6.7 N1:** Voineagu's Supplementary Information PDF also holds per-individual
  tables; only the Table 14 pages, located by their caption, were read into the extract (while the parser was written, p.
  19's first lines and those pages' non-symbol tokens were displayed; no other page), and the PDF was deleted, its sha256
  59f2ded4… kept. **The rule (v6.8, as revised by v6.9 C1; enforced by `src_register/privacy_guard.py` in every register
  builder that reads a workbook — `s_extract.py`, `s_build.py`, `s_build_v66.py`, `fetch_v66.py` — and by `reduce_v68.py`;
  the post-freeze engine `carrier_test.py` prints nothing of a carrier and is unchanged):** a supplement is opened by its
  **sheet names and dimensions only**; a sheet is **identifier-bearing** if its populated width exceeds 50 columns (W; v6.8
  read the dimension tag; since v6.10 the sheet is read by its cells, `ws.reset_dimensions()`), or a cell of its first
  **20** rows (v6.8: 6) carries identifier wording (N: sample / individual / subject / proband / family / donor / patient /
  participant / person / brain bank / biobank / tissue bank / brain / specimen + id / code / number, IID, FID …; v6.10:
  sample / patient / subject / individual / donor / case + a number, "Ind" + a number; v6.9: also a bare subject word —
  Individual, Donor, Subject, Patient, Sample, Proband, Participant, v6.10 also ID and Case — heading a column of numbers or
  of short codes — under a bare "ID" (v6.11) unless every code is an HGNC symbol or a database id other than a year) or an
  identifier-valued token (V, the documented set:
  BrainSpan HSB, NeuroBioBank UMB and brain-bank AN codes and a number joined to a region code such as 9999_QX9 — v6.9: with
  "_", ".", "-" or a space between the parts —, NDA GUIDs, v6.9 also as NDAR_INV… / NDARINV…, SSC and SPARK ids, GTEx and
  TCGA donors, BIDS subject labels, GEO / SRA sample accessions, and a generic code of 1–5 capitals and 3–6 digits that is no
  HGNC approved, previous or alias symbol, UniProt or CCDS id); nothing of an identifier-bearing sheet is printed but its
  name and dimensions (a refusal names the sheet and the rule letter, never a cell); a builder reads from it only the columns
  it declares — exact header texts, or a column rule applied to a header row inside the first 20 rows — every other cell
  reading as None ('' for an .xls), else the read is refused; a sheet with identifier headers is deleted with its file
  without display and only the needed gene-level content is kept; a workbook a builder reads is kept whole only if it holds
  no identifier-bearing sheet (a workbook no builder reads may be kept whole: the three listed above — Parikshak 2016
  Supplementary Table 4 and Voineagu 2011 Supplementary Table 2 for their sha256 alone, SynGO 1.2's annotations.xlsx as a
  member of the pinned SynGO zip, whose genes.xlsx the S-build reads).
  Declared limits: purely numeric identifiers are not detected unless a bare subject word or identifier wording heads their
  column (else only W catches such a sheet); the generic code pattern also flags accession-like tokens (the workbooks listed
  above are of that kind or wide). A bare "ID" or "IDs" header is exempt when every short code below it is an HGNC
  approved / previous / alias symbol or a database id other than a year (v6.11), so a subject table laid out as "ID | Age |
  Sex" over single-letter-plus-digit codes such as S1 … S19 (all HGNC symbols or aliases), "IDs" over such codes, or "ID"
  over dbSNP-shaped codes (rs + digits) reads as clean: from its codes alone the rule cannot tell such a column from a gene
  column headed "ID". No workbook a builder reads or pins is affected — with the exemption disabled, every verdict of
  `privacy_inventory_v68.json` is the same (checked at the freeze).
  Self-test (`privacy_guard.py selftest`; record `register_maps/privacy_guard_selftest.json`):
  **46 / 46** (v6.10: 42 / 42; v6.9: 35 / 35; v6.8: 25 / 25) — **37 planted failures caught** (v6.11: "Sample" over
  S1 … S19, "Donor" over HGNC-colliding B-codes, "Individual" over an HGNC-colliding H-code, a bare "ID" over text years,
  each shown to fail with v6.10's exemption; v6.10: numbered-subject headers, bare "ID" over numbers and bare "Case" over
  short codes, and a sheet of 60 populated columns under a dimension tag "A1:C2" — W, and
  read whole; each shown to fail with its fix disabled; identifier-valued headers of each family, v6.9 also with
  ".", "-" and space separators, a number + region code with "-" or a space, NDA GUIDs written NDAR_INV… and NDARINV…, the
  wording "Brain bank ID", a bare subject word over numbers and over short codes, identifier wording below row 6; a 51-column
  sheet; identifier wording in a header and in a title row; masked reads by header texts and by a column rule; refusals of
  undeclared, missing, duplicated, out-of-zone and invalid declarations), clean gene sheets pass (code-shaped symbols such as
  ZNF804A, LINC00473, KIAA0100; database ids; a 50-column sheet; "Sample size" / "subject to" wording; a three-column sheet
  whose dimension tag reaches column 60; v6.10: a bare "ID" over gene symbols and Ensembl ids), and no HGNC approved /
  previous / alias symbol is flagged by a specific pattern or (v6.10) by the identifier wording.
  Every example and plant is synthetic (v6.9). The audit ARC records the same under session 8.
- **CTD's licence (v6.7 (4)).** CTD's files state its terms: cite CTD; link to its pages; "You must notify CTD, and
  describe your use of our data"; give CTD periodic access to the publication. **CTD must be notified before any result
  that uses its data is published** (Buch's schizophrenia comparator) — on the before-publication list of §6.
- **The source-1 criterion (v6.1, Q1; made positive and keyed by cell id by v6.2, P1 / P2 / P6; tightened by v6.3, O1 … O4
  and O6, and by v6.4, N1 … N4 — the orchestrator under the operator's standing delegation, after the v6 … v6.3 outcomes
  were seen).** One criterion governs **every** source-1 branch — Buch's claims and descriptor cells (MOESM3), the R2 rows,
  the Romero-Garcia families and Pagani's Supplementary Table 3. **(a) Per cell (P2) and naming (v6.3, v6.4 N2 / N3 / N4).**
  Each such cell has an entry in `carrier_tests.json` "source1_lists" → "cells", keyed by its cell id: its target, its name
  alternatives (each a list of phrases that must all appear) and its printed size — Buch: the printed Fig. 5c–h row labels
  (5c–e as the draft transcribes them, 5f–g as printed, 5h as re-read, the asterisk dropped); Pagani: the set names the paper
  prints (Supplementary Table 3's own labels are sealed and unseen); the R2 rows: the Zhang classes (printed size 500; since
  v6.4 N3 "enriched" is one of their phrases, e.g. ["neuron", "enriched"], so a column "Neuron enriched" is named and the word
  is removed as part of the name), the PGC MAGMA list, "*Ribosomal structure"; the families: RG-15 / RG-V23 "second …
  dysregulated", RG-03 / RG-V06 / RG-V07 the pathway names the gated table prints, one run per pathway with one `--name` and
  the table's other printed names as `--rival` (v6.3 O4, v6.4 N4). `carrier_test.py source1 --cell <id>` reads names and size
  from the spec; the TSV builder asserts that the entries are exactly the Buch, Pagani, R2 and family rows (233), that the
  Buch Fig. 5f–h names equal the printed labels, and the family and own-title flags below. A heading names the set iff every
  phrase of one alternative appears in it as whole words; a list is named by its own title; by its own title and the caption
  together **only for the Zhang family**, the caption supplying only "zhang" or "top 500" (v6.4 N2); by the caption alone in a
  table of ≤ 2 titled columns — **except RG-15 / RG-V23, named only when both their phrases stand in the list's own title**
  (v6.4 N2); a heading naming another registered set of the target, one of its rival names (Buch: the 27 PANTHER names its
  labels reach and "structural constituent of ribosome", v6.3 O6) or a family run's rival-only names, whose name contains the
  cell's, names the more specific one. **(b) Positive (P1; v6.3 O2).** A list qualifies only if its sheet name, table title or
  caption, or its own forward-filled title, carries input wording — "gene set(s) used / tested", "input", "definition",
  "membership", "gene set list" ("gene list(s) used / tested" admitted as the same wording). **(c) Refusals (P6; v6.3 O1 / O3;
  v6.4 N1).** Every heading and cell is read with "_" and "." as spaces and the markers at letter boundaries, after every
  phrase of the cell's own alternatives (for a Zhang cell, of every Zhang cell's) is removed and "top N" with N = its printed
  size is exempt. **(1) Sheet-level refusal (v6.4 N1 (1)):** if any cell of the list's sheet (xlsx; the whole csv / txt; for
  docx / PDF the whole table), other than the cells of the lists' own gene runs, or its caption carries statistics wording,
  result wording or a map / subgroup / cohort name, **every list on that sheet is refused** — a cell that reads as one token
  counts only when the **whole token** equals a registered statistics header, result marker or map name (`carrier_tests.json`
  "whole_tokens"; e.g. P, Q, FDR, NES, padj, p-value, ES, OR, Z, ZSTAT, logFC; CORE, TOP, PLS, PLS1, HIT, RESULT, DECODED;
  SG1, SUBGROUP2, HYPO, VALIDATION), a heading of several words by the phrase wording. The v6.3 layers stay as a second
  layer: result wording and map names in the list's own title or the caption; statistics wording there and in every column
  title of its table; no input wording → "not presented as input". **(2) Non-data tokens (v6.4 N1 (2), refined by the
  orchestrator under the standing delegation after its cost was counted):** a single-token cell is never data if it is NA /
  NS / ND / NAN / N/A or if, as read, the **whole token** equals a registered statistics header, result marker or map name —
  not by substring or prefix — so "NES" over "NA", "FDR" over "NS", "P" over "NS", "PLS1", "CORE" or "SG1" in a title stay
  titles and the header zone cannot collapse into the data, while P2RY12, P4HB, S100P, TOP1, DRG1 or a pseudogene "…P1" are
  data. Its cost, counted (`carrier_tests.json` "non_data_cost"; declared): **4 approved HGNC symbols** are never data since
  v6.5 — NES, PLS1, PLS3 and RHO, all protein-coding (NES equals the statistics header NES; PLS1 and PLS3 equal the
  PLS-component result tokens; RHO equals the statistics token "rho" registered by v6.5 M3; v6.4 had 3); a genuine list whose first gene(s) are among them has them fall into its title and is refused as a whole — the
  safe direction; deeper in a list they stay genes. (The first v6.4 draft matched by substring: 10,417 symbols — 10,316
  pseudogenes, 60 protein-coding genes including the microglial P2RY12 — which the refinement returns to data; a plant, a
  Zhang microglia list whose first gene is P2RY12, now qualifies.) **(v6.6, C2)** An approved, previous or alias HGNC symbol
  is never split into pieces (M3's split, below, applies to other tokens only): under v6.5 the pieces of non-symbol-shaped
  symbols made 31 approved and ≈ 870 previous / alias symbols never-data (the re-check's count); read as whole tokens only,
  the cost is the 4 approved symbols above and 14 previous / alias symbols (below), and the 48 approved symbols that are not
  symbol-shaped (e.g. the pseudogene C9orf78P1) are not list members (a list holding one is "not a list of gene symbols") but
  no longer refuse a sheet — a plant (the reviewer's A6): a genuine input sheet whose other set's column holds C9orf78P1
  qualifies. "OR", "ES" and "beta" count as statistics only as a whole heading part; "weighted gene co-expression" is not the
  "weight" marker (v6.4 N3); "enriched" and a threshold "Z > 3" count (v6.3). **(3) The first-filter fixes of v6.5
  (M1 … M4):** a cell of words separated by spaces is a gene list only if no word is NA-like or a registered whole token, it
  carries no statistics / result / map phrase, it is not a generic column title ("GENE", "GENES", "SYMBOL", "GENE SYMBOL",
  "MEMBERS"; **v6.6, C3: tested before the symbol test**, so "GENES" or "SYMBOL" over a list is its title, not its first gene —
  plants: RG-04's 500 genes under either count 500 and qualify), and it does not stand directly over a column of single
  symbols (then it is that column's title) — so the
  all-capitals headers "ADJUSTED P VALUE", "LEADING EDGE GENES", "RANK AT MAX" are titles (M1); every dash — ASCII, U+2010 …
  U+2015, U+2212 — reads as a space and "leading-edge" matches in every form (M2); a single token that is not
  gene-symbol-shaped is also split on punctuation and camelCase and its pieces matched against the whole-token registry
  (pieces "t", "es", "or", "se" excepted), which now also holds LogP, Log(q-value), -log10(P), -log10(FDR), enrichmentScore,
  normalizedEnrichmentScore, enrichmentRatio, GeneRatio, BgRatio, setSize, log2FoldChange, log2err, Bonferroni, Benjamini,
  Enrichment, rho and "false discovery rate" (M3; **v6.6, C6:** also the single tokens r, t, corr and correlation, and the
  whole cells "t value" / "t-value" and "OR (95% CI)" — `carrier_tests.json` "whole_cells", matched as the whole cell, in the
  sheet-level scan and on every column title of the list's table; no gene symbol is a cell of several words — plants, the
  reviewer's A5: a list beside a "t value", "T-VALUE", "Correlation", "r" or "OR (95% CI)" column is refused); NA-like cells
  are dropped from a list's members, and the sheet-wide effect
  of the now 4 never-data symbols (NES, PLS1, PLS3 and — "rho" being registered — RHO) and of the 14 previous / alias symbols
  that equal a registered token (e.g. RANK for TNFRSF11A, T for TBXT, P for OCA2, NS for GNL3 / SPINK5) is declared in
  `carrier_tests.json` "non_data_cost" (a list whose first gene is one of them is refused; one standing outside a list's gene
  run refuses its sheet) (M4). The planted layouts (Enrichr, GSEA, clusterProfiler, Metascape and fGSEA exports in either
  case, "NES" over "NA", "FDR" over "NS" / "NA" / "N/A", "P" over "NS", "SG" over "SG1", "Gene set | PLS1", "Gene set | SG1
  … SG4", a set name over "PLS1" or "CORE", a stacked results table, the four leading-edge forms) are all refused by the
  filter — but since v6.5 no result list rests on the filter alone (corroboration, above). **(d)** Exactly one qualifying
  list (identical copies merge), else the else branch; the printed-size check moved to corroboration (b). Run by
  `carrier_test.py` (`run_source1`; counts and pass / fail only) with 100 planted source-1 cases (§1.11 item 8).
  **The text filter's own gap (v6.3 O1 / v6.4 N1, declared):** a results list passes the filter if its sheet (docx / PDF: its
  table and caption) carries no statistics, result or map wording anywhere and its caption or own title carries input
  wording; markers in other words than those listed are not read. Since v6.5 such a list still has to be corroborated (above).
  **Structural refusals (declared, v6.4 N3), the other direction:** the criterion also refuses genuine input tables that carry
  statistics columns — Zhang's fold enrichment, MAGMA's P / ZSTAT, a DE list's log2FC / FDR, TADA's FDR, SFARI's gene-score —
  lists in long format (one gene per row with its set in another column: no candidate shape reads them), a table of ≥ 3
  titled columns whose set is named only in the caption (caption naming is blocked there), and the first-gene cost of (2).
  The else branch then applies: for Buch's and Pagani's cells sources 2–3; **for the Zhang cells (RG-04, RG-V08, RG-V09,
  RG-N04 … N07) "not auditable (S undefined)"** — so the realised population moves toward §4's lower view. (Since v6.5 RG-N03,
  BUCH-E-Trans-StructConstRibosome-S1 … S4 and RG-15 / RG-V23 are not auditable in any case: nothing can corroborate them;
  since v6.6 also the cells whose comparator is not buildable pre-freeze — 34 in v6.6, 30 since v6.7.)
- **Why some sets go straight to source 3.** Source 1 is the paper's own membership list; where the paper names the
  database whose membership it used, or its carriers can hold only result lists, there is none to look for:
  **Romero-Garcia RG-02, RG-V03 … V05** (GO BP) — the paper names Enrichr as the tool and its GO BP library as the
  membership (draft O13: "Enrichr GO BP / KEGG libraries (version unstated)"; RG-02: "Enrichr GO BP library"), and its
  carriers hold results: MOESM3 is three sheets × 20,737 genes of PLS1 weights (draft O2), and the supplementary tables
  the text cites report enrichment results (Table S2: the enriched KEGG pathways; Table S6: the module enrichments); **Mamat** (GO, KEGG) — the terms come from Metascape (draft §1: "Metascape GO/KEGG … external tools"),
  and its carriers are result files (as the draft infers them, §1): Additional file 2 the gene terms of its decoded lists
  (URG / DRG, selected on its own maps), file 3 the enrichment results, file 4 the PPI network, file 1 the supplementary
  PDF; **Rasero** — the categories are
  WebGestalt's GO BP / Reactome sets (Key Resources Table: WebGestalt; draft O11), and its repository lists
  (`results/desikan/Subtype_*_r2_gen_association_results.csv`) are per-gene association results on its own maps (draft O2).
- **Conditional S (v6, R2; the orchestrator under the operator's standing delegation, decided after the v5 S-build
  outcome was seen; its criterion restated by v6.1 Q1).** A set that sources 2–3 leave undefined, but whose source 1 (the
  paper's own list, figure or table) could lie inside a gated carrier, is registered as "S from <named gated carrier>
  (source 1) if present, by the source-1 criterion above; else not auditable (S undefined)", built under that gate by
  these rules. Rows (v6.5): RG-04, RG-V08, RG-V09 and RG-N04 … N07 (Zhang 2014 cell classes; Romero-Garcia's mixed
  supplements, G1; corroborated only by the printed size 500) and the Romero-Garcia KEGG families RG-03, RG-V06, RG-V07
  (their cells are the KEGG pathways a gated table names — source 1, corroborated against the pathway of the KEGG library
  chosen by the rule above, else that pathway; not auditable if the gated table naming the cells is absent or no name reaches
  that library, v6.2 P7 (a)). **Since v6.5 no longer conditional** — nothing can corroborate their source 1, so they are
  "not auditable (S undefined: source 1 uncorroborated)": RG-N03 (the PGC MAGMA list), BUCH-E-Trans-StructConstRibosome-S1
  … S4 (the printed label "*Ribosomal structure" reaches no PANTHER term, §1.11 item 9, and no size is printed) and RG-15 /
  RG-V23 (the second dysregulated list, identified only in the supplement). (v6.5 also listed here 36 Buch cells "whose set
  the draft gives no source for" — wrong, v6.6 C5: each has a cited source and a comparator, or is not buildable, above.)
  m is stated as a range (§4): its lower bound counts none of the conditional claims, its upper bound every one (if every such
  carrier qualifies) — since v6.5 no claim in m is conditional, so the two bounds are equal — and the composition marks the
  conditional rows provisional (§4's lower view counts them not auditable). The
  criterion cannot be met for LEY-M1 … M4 and SEE-M01 … M11 (Kang modules: the only gated objects of Leyhausen and
  Seelemeyer are NeuroVault images, and their supplements, read before the register, publish no module membership) and
  PRE-B01 … B14 (FUMA's sets are FUMA's; Pretzsch publishes no gene names): these stay unconditionally "not auditable (S
  undefined)" — exceptions to R2's row list confirmed by the orchestrator (v6.1 Q5; §9).
- **Pinned definitions** (per target in §2–§3 and in the TSV): GO sets = annotations to the term or any descendant over
  is_a and part_of, all evidence codes except ND, NOT-qualified annotations excluded; CSEA / SEA sets = the pSI tables
  of `pSI.data` **1.0** (Xu et al. 2014; not on CRAN; the Dougherty laboratory's distribution, pinned by URL and sha256
  in the pre-freeze S-build, §1.11 item 2) at pSI < .05: the **human BrainSpan developmental table** (columns = region ×
  period) for Benkarim and Park, cells named by region {Cortex, Thalamus, Striatum, Cerebellum} and CSEA period
  {Neonatal-Early Infancy, Early Childhood, Adolescence, Young Adulthood}; the **human brain-region table** for Mamat's SEA
  regions {Cerebellum, Cortex, Thalamus, Hippocampus}, read by the S-build as `pSI.data` human$young.adulthood (an S-build
  reading, v6 R3); the **mouse cell-type table** (TRAP) for Mamat's cell types {Pnoc+ cortical neurons, cholinergic
  striatal neurons} — **not auditable (definition not applicable: v4 pinned a mouse table without an orthology rule)**
  (v6, R3). A cell's column is found by case-insensitive token match of its region (or cell type) and period, read by
  the S-build as "the query's tokens are a subset of the column's tokens" (an S-build reading, v6 R3); exactly one
  matching column is required, else "not auditable (S undefined)"; Gandal 2018 (Science 362, eaat8127) gene-level ASD
  differential expression table, ASD FDR < .05 with log2FC < 0 (down) or > 0 (up); Parikshak 2016 (Nature 540) module
  assignments (their supplementary table); Velmeshev 2019 (Science 364) Data S4, union over cell types at q < .05;
  Polioudakis 2019 (Neuron 103) cluster-enriched genes table, all rows per cluster; Kang 2011 (Nature 478) module
  membership table; SFARI Gene: the earliest release whose score-1 count equals the paper's stated count, else the last
  release before the paper's submission (v6.7 (2) / N2: the releases the Wayback Machine holds count, each read by its served
  label; Liu → 03-28-2024, Xing → 07-12-2026 in a state 25 days after its date (its first journal submission, 2026-08-31;
  v6.8), "state substituted"; the resolution rule's
  table and the uniform archive rule above); KEGG: the release rules above — Leyhausen Enrichr KEGG_2021_Human ("release
  substituted": the paper's 2021-12-14 retrieval is not obtainable), Vairam / Roy Enrichr KEGG_2021_Human (R12),
  Romero-Garcia Enrichr KEGG_2015 or KEGG_2016 by the rule above (under G1), Mamat KEGG REST as captured 2024-09-16 (under
  G1; v6.7 (2); v6.1 … v6.6: Enrichr KEGG_2026, "release substituted"); GO BP
  (Romero-Garcia): Enrichr GO_Biological_Process_2015 (R12); MSigDB C5 GOBP 2023.1.Hs for Pretzsch (the first release with
  "MONOATOMIC" term names); **Buch's GO-slim cells** (v6.1, Q7): Buch's own list in MOESM3 by the source-1 criterion (under
  G1), else the fallback **built before the freeze** — the PANTHER 16.0 GO-slim term (the release the paper names;
  `PANTHERGOslim.obo` pinned) that the printed figure label reaches by the name rule, its members **PANTHER 16.0's own
  gene-to-GO-slim annotation as served** (v6.2, P3; the orchestrator under the operator's standing delegation, after the v6.1
  outcome was seen): `PTHR16.0_human` from data.pantherdb.org (sequence_classifications/16.0; no identifier sent; sha256
  d605cf60…), the genes whose row lists the term in its GO-slim MF / BP / CC column, no propagation added, HGNC id → HGNC
  (else Ensembl / Entrez / UniProt id, else the symbol); a named tool's library as served (the R12 logic), sizes in columns
  (27 sets; §1.11 item 2). v6.1's version — the same GO ids annotated at GO release 2021-11-16 by the GO rule — is kept only
  as a descriptor of overlap; **Buch's Fig. 5c–e sets** (v6.6, B2): Buch's own list in MOESM3 by the source-1 criterion and
  corroboration (under G1), else the comparator built before the freeze (the table above); **Pagani**
  (v6.1, Q1): the list Supplementary Table 3 presents as the set's input membership (its decoded genes never qualify) and
  corroborated, else the comparator built before the freeze (v6.6, B2; the table above).
- **S-build runs before the freeze** (verification finding NF9; no map is opened, so it exposes nothing) for every set
  that needs no map or gated carrier: membership, size, HGNC drops, < 5 / > 1,000 outcomes and sha256 go to
  `arcs/audit/register_sets/` and its manifest is frozen with this register, so no frozen-11 claim turns "not
  auditable" after the freeze by surprise. Sets inside a gated carrier (e.g. Pagani Supplementary Table 3, Buch MOESM3)
  are built under the target's gate by the same rules. A rule that does not determine a set → "not auditable (S
  undefined)" (or R2's conditional form), recorded before the freeze.

### 1.7 Maps: attribution, analysis class, re-parcellation (pinned now)
- **NeuroVault attribution** (amendment 4 §0: provenance by "collection and image metadata"; the rule was written after
  the drafts had listed the candidates' metadata, declared, §6 item 2). An image is **attributed** to the paper iff (a)
  the collection or image owner is an author, (b) where `number_of_subjects` is set it equals the paper's analysed N, (c)
  the image names or contrasts match the paper's maps, (d) nothing in the metadata contradicts the paper (N, modality,
  map type, method), and (e) where the paper cites a repository or collection, that copy governs. **Version rule:** the
  copy the paper cites; else the latest upload dated before the paper's publication; within a collection, the image
  without NeuroVault-side or extra smoothing ("Raw"). Every attributed map carries "attribution: metadata (version
  inferred)" unless the paper cites the copy. Attributed: Pagani (GitHub, paper-cited; = NeuroVault 14698 by byte size),
  Benkarim 8667 (images 404555 `sdistRaw`, 404554 `edistRaw`; later than collections 8655 / 8657 / 8658), Park 743 (image
  396720; 319367 a descriptor), Leyhausen 12301. Rejected: Seelemeyer 13397 (N 535 against 428), Pretzsch 12188 / 12203
  (PCA maps with a feature the paper does not use). The visual-match route of amendment 4 §0 is **not used** for any
  target (it is subjective and would expose maps); Seelemeyer's Fig. 3A renders were seen incidentally and compared with
  nothing.
- **Form:** a NeuroVault image uploaded as a surface (`data_origin: surface`) is used as its left-hemisphere fsaverage
  GIFTI; one uploaded as a volume is used as the volume.
- **Analysis class** (amendment 5 §4.3; prereg §2 left + midline primary, bilateral a multiverse cell):
  native DK cortical → **dk-ctx**; cortical surface, vertex or other cortical parcellations → re-parcellated onto
  **dk-ctx**; whole-brain voxel maps → re-parcellated onto **V-AAL** (the planted class covering cortex, subcortex and
  cerebellum); DK cortex + subcortex → **V-DK**; a subcortex-only map → V-DK's subcortical parcels, single class,
  "calibration extrapolated (class)"; MIST64 → native MIST64 (§3.6), "calibration extrapolated (class)".
- **Re-parcellation rules** (labelled "re-parcellated"):
  - *volume → class atlas:* the class atlas NIfTI (abagen `atlas-desikankilliany.nii.gz`, sha256 0a28c93f…, for V-DK /
    dk-ctx; PCP `aal_roi_atlas.nii.gz`, sha256 40b1edfa…, for V-AAL) resampled to the map's grid by nearest neighbour;
    parcel value = mean over its voxels with a finite non-zero value; a parcel with < 50 % of its voxels finite and
    non-zero is dropped and reported (A3's T2 rule);
  - *fsaverage surface → dk-ctx:* FreeSurfer's fsaverage `lh.aparc.annot` (the FreeSurfer 7.4.1 distribution file,
    sha256 recorded at download); parcel value = mean over the label's vertices with a finite value; labels `unknown`
    and `corpuscallosum` excluded; DK labels matched to the abagen DK names;
  - *parcel values → dk-ctx:* Glasser-360 through `HCP-MMP1_on_MNI152_ICBM2009a_nlin.nii.gz` (Horn 2016, figshare
    3501911); Schaefer-400 (17 networks) through nilearn's `fetch_atlas_schaefer_2018(n_rois=400, yeo_networks=17,
    resolution_mm=1)`; the source atlas and abagen's DK atlas are compared on the source atlas's native grid (DK
    resampled to it by nearest neighbour); each source parcel is assigned to the DK label holding the majority of its
    voxels (a parcel whose majority is outside every DK cortical label is dropped and reported); the DK value is the
    voxel-count-weighted mean of its assigned parcels; left hemisphere only;
  - *DK-308 → dk-ctx (Romero-Garcia):* each sub-parcel nests in its Desikan–Killiany parent by name prefix
    (`lh_bankssts_part1` → bankssts); DK value = the vertex-count-weighted mean of its sub-parcels, counts from
    `lh.500.aparc.annot` in the paper's subParcellation repository (commit e351b3cf); left hemisphere only;
  - *Power spheres → V-AAL (Buch):* each sphere to the AAL label containing its centroid, using the ROI coordinates of
    the paper's own table; label value = mean of its spheres (a sphere whose centroid falls outside every AAL label is
    dropped and reported); if the coordinates are not in the carrier → "not auditable (ROI coordinates unpublished)";
  - bilateral-average maps (one value per DK region) are placed on the left parcels (as Patel, Giacomel and Dear did).
- R²_grad, R²_|x| and tissue classes use the analysis class's centroids and classes (amendment 5 §4.3).

### 1.8 Status vocabulary and precedence
auditable · **conditional** (auditable iff a named public carrier, opened under the target's gate, holds the named
object as numeric values with labels for every parcel or gene the claim needs; partial or figure-only content does not
qualify) · **S conditional** (v6, R2: S from a named gated carrier if it defines the set, else not auditable (S
undefined); §1.6) · not auditable (reason) · not testable / not testable as stated · deferred · published-null ·
descriptor · not an ASD claim · out of scope (recorded, not registered). A claim may carry several flags (e.g. not
auditable and not testable as stated); all are reported, and its population membership follows §1.2. **Precedence (v6,
R8; the orchestrator under the operator's standing delegation, decided after the v5 outcome was seen):** in the
composition of the population (§1.2) a claim is counted once, by the first flag it carries in this order — **deferred >
not testable (< 5) > not auditable > not testable as stated > component-level > no calibrated test** (deferred claims are
listed apart and "not testable (< 5)" ones are outside the population, so inside it the order starts at "not
auditable"); every flag stays reported. A claim whose S is conditional (R2) is counted by its other flags and marked
provisional (the "lower view" of §4 counts it as not auditable). Beside the composition, the cross-tab of
component-level-in-form × not auditable (§4) shows every claim of component form, including those counted as not
auditable. **Carrier test** (pre-registered per conditional target in the TSV before the freeze): the exact label list,
the required count and a numeric parse of every value; the extraction script prints only pass / fail and counts.

### 1.9 Null counts, seeds, multiplicity
- Verdict legs: 10,000 per leg (prereg §4.2–4.3; amendment 3 §3): S1c, S2c (exact enumeration when patterns ≤ 10,000),
  G1, G3m, G6s (exact when 2^rank ≤ 10,000), G6l over every eligible seed (exact; 10,000 sampled if more are eligible);
  component Level 0 10,000 permutations; re-selection loops at the same count.
- Descriptor legs (amendment 3 §2: reported for every claim): G2, G4, G5 at 10,000; J1 with K = 1,000 G2 sets and 10,000
  surrogates of each adopted type (prereg §4.4); the naive joint null 10,000; T_abs legs 10,000. Donor robustness
  (prereg §3.1: six leave-one-donor-out matrices; sign kept in ≥ 5 of 6) and the mini-multiverse (gene_norm ∈ {srs,
  zscore}; norm_matched ∈ {True, False}; bilateral) for every surviving claim.
- Seeds: `numpy.random.SeedSequence([20260925, 100 + target number, row number of the cell in claims_register.tsv,
  leg, map index, cell index])` (map index 0 for a single-map cell, Mamat's two-cohort claims and descriptor families
  0 = ABIDE I, 1 = ABIDE II, Romero-Garcia's validation families 0 = Val1, 1 = Val2; cell index 0 for a single cell and
  1 … n for the cells of a family, and for the register-numbered units SEG-C3-D01 … D71, PRE-DG-01 … 06 and RA-N3-01 …
  10, in the order their defining table lists them (as the TSV numbers them); legs 0 S1c, 1 S2c, 2 G1, 3 G3m, 4 G6s,
  5 G6l, 8 permutation / label, 10 re-selection, 11 G2, 12 G4, 13 G5, 14 J1, 15 naive joint, 21 T_abs S1c, 22 T_abs
  S2c, 23 T_abs G1, 24 T_abs G3m, 25 T_abs G6l.
- m (prereg §4.6 resolution check, kept for the descriptive BH) is fixed by the TSV (§4) as a range (v6, R2): its lower
  bound counts the claims whose S is unconditional, its upper bound also those whose S is conditional on a gated carrier;
  the upper bound is ≤ 250 claims with a verdict, so 1/10,001 < .05/m (the builder checks it).

### 1.10 After the freeze (nothing is decided later)
(0) Before the freeze: the S-build (§1.6), the MIST64 classes and centroids (§3.6), the carrier tests (§1.8), and the
identity of every gated map by URL, byte size and (for git copies) blob id, since prereg §5.2's file hash can only be
taken at download (declared deviation; a mismatch at download → "not auditable (map changed)"). **The identity fields
compared at download (v6, R13):** byte size plus git blob id for git copies; byte size plus the served MD5 where the
server gives a plain MD5 (an ETag of 32 hex digits, or a repository checksum declared MD5 — Apollo's DSpace listing);
byte size alone otherwise (NeuroVault serves no ETag); a copy already on disk is checked by its sha256 (every gated map's
TSV row names its fields). (1) After the
freeze, ungated targets. (2) Under each gate: downloads, carriers, extraction, maps, gated sets — by scripts that
print nothing but counts and hashes: carriers are inspected by header and sheet names only; a carrier holding
participant-level data has only the needed group-level object extracted once, then is deleted (as Pretzsch's
Supplement 2); nothing individual-level enters git, prompts, transcripts or logs. Every object is hashed into
`register_maps/` and `register_sets/` manifests and committed. (3) The audit runner, with planted cases per T form (a
planted set on a synthetic map survives; a random set does not; a pooled-sign plant defeats the signed T), reviewed by
asdg-skeptic before any claim runs. (4) The run in gate order. (5) Results: every registered cell with flags and labels.
A registered definition that cannot be applied as written → "not auditable (definition not applicable: <why>)", never a
redefinition without a frozen amendment.

gated or deferred carrier, map or NeuroVault image was opened; listing-level identity only. The files below are frozen
with this register through `arcs/audit/register_freeze_manifest.json` (sha256 of every file; written at the TSV rebuild).

§1.10) and in §9. Where a number below repeats one of item 11's block or §4's block, the block governs.

   the synthetic-PDF plants run, a misaligned label / value case among them, which must be caught. Done before the freeze

   matplotlib 3.11.2): a 400-parcel named table over 11 pages passes, an index table whose page text names the 17-network
   order file passes, a table whose values are shifted one row against its labels fails ("non-numeric values"), and a
   one-page dk34 table passes. (A first plant with clipped label cells failed as "labels missing"; the plant's column widths
   were fixed, and a clipped carrier would fail the same way — conservatively.) **v6.1 Q1:** the source-1 criterion is
   pinned as "source1_lists" and its plants run: a membership list under the set's name qualifies; a leading-edge list in a
   results-table row under the set's name, a column titled with the set's name and "leading edge", and the overlap genes of
   an enrichment-results row are refused; a 500-symbol list against the printed size 500 qualifies, a 499-symbol one goes

   source-1 plants run by cell id (names and printed size read from "source1_lists" → "cells"; 233 entries: Buch 208,
   Pagani 12, Romero-Garcia 13). v6.1's plants were restated where the positive criterion requires it (their input lists
   now sit in sheets named "Gene sets used in enrichment" / "Gene set definitions"; the Enrichr results row is now refused as
   not presented as input) and all are caught; new: the same membership list in a sheet with no input wording → refused;
   the reviewer's three layouts — a sheet "Genes driving the enrichment of each gene set (subgroup 1)" with one column per
   set, a column "Ribosomal structure core genes", a sheet "Subgroup 1" listing the set's genes with the NES / padj table on
   another sheet — each refused; the Pagani layout — "ASD DE genes" beside "Hypo decoded genes" under "Gene lists used in
   enrichment" qualifies for PAG-C1, the upper-case title "SYNGO" is a title and qualifies for PAG-C4, and in a "Gene set |
   Genes" table the "TGF-beta signaling" row and PAG-C7's row qualify; by cell id, RG-04 (printed size 500) qualifies on
   "Neuron-specific genes (top 500)" and RG-N05 finds no list; a docx table under a caption paragraph with input wording and a
   PDF table under such a caption line qualify for RG-03 / RG-V06 with the printed name passed, and the PDF table under a
   caption naming a PLS result is refused. **v6.3 (O1 … O4, O6):** the reviewer's GAP1 (an Enrichr export Term | Overlap |
   P-value | Adjusted P-value | Odds Ratio | Combined Score | Genes on a sheet "Enrichment of the gene sets tested"), GAP2
   ("Supplementary Table 5. GSEA of the gene sets used"; Gene set | Subgroup | NES | padj | Genes), P1b (clusterProfiler
   Description | NES | p.adjust | core_enrichment under "Gene sets tested"), GAP3 (a table under "KEGG pathways enriched in
   the input gene list (PLS1 Z > 3)") and GAP4 (a "top_genes" column) are each refused, as are six columns "Ribosomal
   structure GSEA_results / SG1 / hit_gene / urg_list / pls1 gene / PLS1" (all six) and PAG1 (Table S3's index title as the
   caption); PAG3 (a merged super-header "Gene lists used in enrichment analyses" over the input columns) qualifies for PAG-C1;
   ZH1 ("Top 500 cell-type-specific genes (Zhang 2014)" over one column per class, in a sheet named as input) qualifies for
   RG-04, RG-N04, RG-N05, RG-N06 and RG-N07; RB1 (a list headed "Structural constituent of ribosome") is not "Ribosome" and
   RB2 ("Ribosome" beside it) qualifies with its own list; an RG-03 run with `--name` "Ribosome_Homo_sapiens_hsa03010"
   qualifies and an RG-03 run given two pathway names is refused. Every one of these 17 plants is misclassified by the v6.2
   engine (a negative control run on the same files before the v6.3 rules were adopted: GAP1, GAP2, P1b, GAP3, GAP4 and RB1
   qualified; the marker columns, PAG3, ZH1, RB2 and the suffix run went to the else branch; the two-name run was not refused).
   **v6.4 (N1 … N4):** refused — "NES" over "NA", "FDR" over "NS" (xlsx), "FDR" over "NA" (csv), "FDR" over "N/A" (docx),
   "P" over "NS", "SG" over "SG1" (long format), RG-03 "Pathway | P | FDR | Genes" with FDR "NA", "Gene set | PLS1", "Gene set |
   SG1 … SG4", a set name over "PLS1" and over "CORE", and a results table stacked under a neutral list; not named (the
   reviewer's two N2 plants) — RG-15 with "second" only in the caption and "dysregulated" in the column title, RG-V23 with both
   phrases only in the sheet name; qualify — RG-04 on "Neuron enriched" beside the other classes' "… enriched" columns, RG-15 on
   "Second list of genes dysregulated in autism (weighted gene co-expression network analysis)", PAG-C1 on Pagani input lists on
   a sheet of their own; I7 — RG-03 "Ribosome" beside "Ribosome biogenesis in eukaryotes" is ambiguous without rival names and
   resolves to its own row with the table's other names as `--rival`; after the whole-token refinement of N1 (2), a Zhang
   microglia input list whose first gene is P2RY12 qualifies for RG-N06, and the twelve N1 layouts, "P" over "NS" among them,
   are still refused. **Three earlier plants flip** under the sheet-level
   rule: the v6.2 Pagani layout (PAG-C1 beside "Hypo decoded genes"; PAG-C4's "SYNGO") and v6.3's PAG3 (the merged
   super-header beside decoded columns) are now refused ("decoded" / "hypo" on the sheet) — they had qualified. Negative
   control (the v6.3 engine and spec, then the last commit, on the same files): ten of the twelve N1 layouts qualified and two
   ("FDR" over "N/A" in docx, "Gene set | SG1 … SG4") went to the else branch by ambiguity, not refusal; both N2 plants
   qualified; the two N3 layouts were refused or not named; the Pagani inputs-only layout qualified (a positive control); I7
   without rivals was ambiguous. **v6.5 ((1) … (4)):** the reviewer's layouts, refused by the text filter — a capital-letter Enrichr export ("P VALUE",
   "ADJUSTED P VALUE", "ODDS RATIO", "COMBINED SCORE"), a capital-letter fGSEA export with multi-word headers ("ADJUSTED P
   VALUE", "RANK AT MAX", "LEADING EDGE GENES") and one with single-word capitals, capital-letter GAP2 and Pagani layouts, the
   four leading-edge forms ("leading-edge", "leading‐edge", "leading–edge", "leadingEdge"), clusterProfiler's camelCase
   "GeneRatio" / "BgRatio" and Metascape's "LogP" / "-log10(FDR)"; corroboration, on the final result — on Buch's pinned
   fallback "secretory vesicle" (112 HGNC genes in V-AAL): a leading-edge-like subset of 30 in an input-worded sheet passes the
   text filter and fails (Jaccard .268), the list equal to the set qualifies (Jaccard 1; also with NA cells among its genes,
   which are dropped), 89 of the 112 (Jaccard .795) fails, and a genuine all-capitals input sheet ("SECRETORY VESICLE" over its
   genes) qualifies; RG-04's 500 symbols qualify on the printed size, 501 and 499 fail; "*Ribosomal structure" (no external
   definition, no printed size) goes to the else branch though the filter admits its list; PAG-C1 is not corroborated without
   `--external` and qualifies with the sources 2–3 set supplied. Negative control (the v6.4 engine and spec, then the last
   commit, on the same files): the capital Enrichr and multi-word fGSEA exports, the capital Pagani layout, the three
   dashed leading-edge forms and clusterProfiler's camelCase layout qualified; the leading-edge subset, the Jaccard-.79 list
   and the all-capitals positive control all qualified (no corroboration existed). Of the 56 earlier plants checked on the
   filter, 18 are admitted by it; 8 of these are corroborated (the Zhang printed-size cases), the other 10 go to the else
   branch (no external definition or size, a synthetic list against a pinned set, or no --external).
   **v6.6 (B1, C1, C2, C3, C6), on the final result:** B1 — PAG-C1's genuine list (its comparator's 4,401 HGNC genes in the V-AAL universe
   under "Gene lists used in enrichment analyses") qualifies against the pinned Gandal 2022 comparator; with another committed

   its file hash pinned: the members' hash differs), with the file missing, with malformed JSON (its bytes pinned, so the
   parser is reached), with one byte appended, and with a class the set has no universe for, it is refused each time with
   the reason; RG-03's recipe qualifies the list of the pathway its rule picks ("Ribosome" beside "Spliceosome": KEGG_2016
   hsa03010) and refuses a changed KEGG_2015 pin and a name that reaches no pathway; a cell whose comparator is not buildable
   (BUCH-E-ASDdown-S1) goes to the else branch though the filter admits its list. C1 — 400 distinct approved symbols plus
   100 repeats (500 rows) and 500 arbitrary symbol-shaped tokens fail RG-04's printed 500 (the reviewer's A9, A10); 500
   approved symbols plus one unmapped token qualify (the count is of approved symbols); the Zhang size plants now use 500
   approved symbols of the dk-ctx universe. C3 — RG-04's 500 genes under a "GENES" or a "SYMBOL" header row count 500 and
   qualify. C2 — the reviewer's A6 (C9orf78P1 in the other column) qualifies. C6 — the reviewer's A5 family ("t value",
   "T-VALUE", "Correlation", "r", "OR (95% CI)" beside the list) is refused. The declared residual — the reviewer's A7, an 85
   % subset — qualifies (Jaccard .857). Negative control (the v6.5 engine and spec of 61fb674 on the same files): a
   `--external` file holding the list itself qualified PAG-C1, a missing `--external` file crashed the engine
   (FileNotFoundError); A9 and A10 qualified; RG-04 under "GENES" / "SYMBOL" went to the else branch (the header counted as a
   gene: 501); A6 was refused (sheet level: the piece "P"); the five A5 layouts qualified; A7 qualified (unchanged: the
   declared residual).
   **v6.7 (N1, (1), N3), on the final result:** v6.6's not-buildable plant moves to BUCH-E-ASDup-S1 (ASDdown is built): the
   filter admits its list, the else branch applies; N1 — BUCH-E-ASDdown-S1's list equal to its comparator (Voineagu Table

   an environment failure is recorded by its exception type only (an OS error's path differs between runs).
   **v6.8 (B S1), on the final result:** v6.7's N1 plants move to the near-miss view — BUCH-E-ASDdown-S1's list equal to its
   near-miss comparator (Voineagu Table 14, 370 genes in V-AAL) qualifies in the view (`--view near_miss`) and goes to

## 2. The frozen-11 targets (prereg §5.1)

Numbers are target numbers (seeds, TSV); per-cell fields are in the TSV and the drafts.

### 2.1 Segura 2024/25 (segura2024symptommapping) — target 1 — ungated (§0)
- **SEG-C1** MFG map 790618 × Table S4 (1,046; the paper's n) and **SEG-C3a–g** (the seven GO terms the text and Fig. 4
  name; S = GO term (release 2024-01-17) ∩ Table S4; Table S7's other 71 terms descriptor cells, named in the
  pre-freeze S-build from the already-read table with GO IDs by the name rule (§1.1; all 78 terms by (a), exact name),
  the 77-row / 78-term parse repaired, an unresolved name → "not auditable (S undefined)"): **not testable as
  stated** — the decoded list pools positive and negative associations (Supplement §6: "positive and negative t both
  kept"). Signed T and T_abs computed as descriptors on V-AAL (re-parcellated; voxels = 0 missing). Level gene null only
  (uniform). Reproduction descriptive (NeuroVault decoder, `mask=full`, FDR q < .05, both signs; OR vs 1.6).
  **SEG-C3b, SEG-C3d** (3 columns of S in the class universe, §1.11 item 2) are also "not testable (< 5)": out of the
  population (§1.2), no T computed.
- **SEG-C2** control map 781986 (as the text names it; 801380's matching sphere recorded, not run): published-null, also
  pooled → not testable as stated, descriptors only.
- Q1: both folders kept. Q2: the Pagani rule's deferral (until A3's hypotheses froze) is met; ungated (§0); Segura and
  Pagani share the CMI collection (dependency recorded).
  Q3 Table S4. Q10: no author contact.
- Focal (per-row descriptor): SEG-C1 (a decoded-list form; its status is "not testable as stated").

### 2.2 Patel 2021 (patel2021virtual) — target 2 — gate G2 (ENIGMA-ASD row)
- y = eTable 4, column Estimate (34 left DK regions), transcribed from the supplement PDF p. 26 by a non-printing script
  with read-back; class **dk-ctx**; level gene null only (uniform; within a 2,511-gene pool).
- **PAT-C1** (focal, Key Points / Results "Virtual Histology…"), **PAT-C2**, **PAT-C3**: set-vs-map, direction −; §4.1
  check against the mean bicor on our matrix (−.201 / −.226 / −.212).
- **PAT-C4:** not auditable (the selection re-run amendment 3 §2 requires needs the unpublished 2,511-gene reference
  pool). PAT-X1–X3 out of scope. Six null panels: descriptor cells, two-sided.

### 2.3 Giacomel 2026 (giacomel2026cartography) — target 3 — gate G2 (ENIGMA-ASD row, published-null for ASD)
- Map: xlsx `Thickness` / `Subcortical` at repository commit 93de089b614f72bda25f441518ad04b1e938fb9b (sha256 prefix
  15101878fd0cae37; the version behind the published ASD tables); its ASD column compared with c022a29's, any difference
  recorded, 93de089 governing.
- **GIA-C07 … C18** (up-only / down-only): published-null cells, two-sided, T = r(y, unweighted set score) (declared
  deviation from the |z|-weighted mean; the weights are all positive). Cortical cells dk-ctx (level spatial null only);
  subcortical cells single-class V-DK subcortex (level other: bootstrap of 7 values), **resolution-limited by
  construction** (G6s: rank 6 → 64 patterns → floor 2/65 ≈ .031 ≥ .025; two-sided) — reported as resolution-limited;
  their published ρ "not reproduced (documented source defect: misaligned structures)", the misalignment reproduced as a
  descriptor. Q4: supplement values registered, shipped values recorded (C07, C11, C12); where the supplement row
  carries another cell's value (C11: Table S2's −.093 is the shipped subcortical value; C12: .449 is the combined value),
  the §4.1 comparator is the shipped value (C11 −.1325; C12 −.0936), flagged "supplement row carries another cell's
  value".
- **Combined-scheme cells C01–C06 and post-mortem-DEG cells C19–C24:** not testable as stated (signed-weight scores).
  Descriptors: the paper's weighted GEDAR map ρ with y (C01–C06 eda33ab maps; C19–C24 rebuilt with the paper's code
  `literature_gwas_analysis.R` on the authors' AHBA matrix, labelled "rebuilt from the paper's code"), our class-
  stratified site leg on ρ, and G1–G5 and J1 with S's weights carried over (each null set receives S's weight vector in
  a random order).
- **GIA-MDD (prereg §8 registered outcome), enumerated now from Supplementary Tables S1–S3** (published p_spin < .05):
  combined 1 % / 5 % / 10 % cortical (ρ .531 / .547 / .529; p .003 / .003 / .005) → not testable as stated, delivered as
  the descriptors above (the prereg §8 question "does it survive G1–G5 and J1" is answered by those descriptor legs, with
  weights carried over, and the site leg); **GIA-MDD-U1** up-only 1 % cortical (ρ .396, p .031, FDR .093) and
  **GIA-MDD-D10** down-only 10 % cortical (ρ −.424, p .017, FDR .051): set-vs-map claims on the MDD map of the same xlsx,
  direction + / −, S = the repository's `results/MDD/MDD_top_pos_genes_thr_1.tsv` and `MDD_top_neg_genes_thr_10.tsv` at
  e045bc5 (as the ASD files); the combined MDD cells carry ids GIA-MDD-C1 / C5 / C10, sets
  `results/MDD/MDD_top_genes_thr_{1,5,10}.tsv` at e045bc5 and the eda33ab MDD GEDAR maps (paths asserted against the
  repository's tree listing before the freeze). The MDD post-mortem-DEG result:
  descriptor cells for every threshold (1, 5, 10 %) × compartment (cortical, subcortical) with the MDD-DEG set and again
  with the ASD-DEG set the code writes into the MDD file (12 cells), reproducing the flagged defect. Table S1's MDD
  combined ρ at 1 % and 10 % (.531 / .529) and R2's reproduction (.5287 / .5310) differ in their third decimals in
  opposite directions — a labelling swap in one of the two, recorded.
- Every subcortical cell (the combined and DEG ones included) carries "not reproduced (documented source defect)".
- Species legs on the §3.1 universe (the paper's AHBA ∩ HPA pool recorded). H-MAGMA (X1): not auditable (list and ASD
  value unpublished), level other (unstated). X2, X3 out of scope.

### 2.4 Romero-Garcia 2019 (romerogarcia2019synaptic) — target 4 — gate G1
- **Conditional** (v1 had "not auditable"): the mixed supplements — MOESM1 / MOESM2 docx, the Apollo record's SI `.txt`
  and `Figures_Tables.zip`, still behind G1 — are opened when G1 opens and searched, by the pre-registered carrier test,
  for a DK-308 ΔCT table (discovery; Val1, Val2 separately) holding the **152 left** labels with values (the route uses
  only the left hemisphere; a left-only carrier qualifies; §1.11 item 8); the MOESM3 "Discovery" sheet gives the PLS1
  weights.
- **RG-01** (discovery PLS1 component claim; level other: non-spatial permutation; §4.1 comparator r(PLS1 score, y)
  against the published r = .32) and **RG-V01, RG-V02** (validation components): component level, auditable iff the
  carrier holds the corresponding ΔCT; route dk-ctx by the DK-308 nesting rule (§1.7).
- **RG-V24, RG-V25** (ranking-only lists selected on the claim maps): not auditable (definition not applicable) — the
  paper does not state how the weights of its three (V24) or two (V25) datasets are combined into one ranking, and V25's
  N is unset, so the selected-list re-selection cannot be written without inventing it.
- **RG-02 … RG-15 and RG-V03 … RG-V23:** the paper pools both signs ("the sign of a weight was not informative") → **not
  testable as stated** whatever the carrier holds; descriptors (signed T, T_abs) if the map is found. **RG-02, RG-V03,
  RG-V04** ("Synaptic Transmission") and **RG-V05** ("Membrane depolarization"): S = the Enrichr GO_Biological_Process_2015
  gene list of GO:0007268 / GO:0051899 (v6, R12: the release the paper used; §1.6), reached by (c) of the name rule (case;
  §1.11 item 2) — no longer S undefined (v5 read RG-02, V03, V04 as not auditable), so they count as not testable as
  stated. **RG-04, RG-V08, RG-V09** (Zhang 2014 neuron-specific genes): S conditional on the mixed supplements (v6, R2:
  source 1 if present by the pre-registered criterion and corroborated by its printed size 500, v6.5; else not auditable (S
  undefined)); counted as not testable as stated, provisionally (§1.8; §4's lower view counts them not auditable). RG-13, RG-14 (depletions): not testable as
  stated. **RG-03 / RG-V06 / RG-V07** (KEGG families; v6.1 Q2, v6.2 P5 / P7 (a)): the cells are the pathways Table S2 /
  S3 / S4 name; per cell S = the list the carrier presents as that pathway's input membership (source-1 criterion), else the
  pathway the printed name reaches by the name rule in the Enrichr KEGG library chosen by §1.6 (KEGG_2015 or KEGG_2016: the
  one reaching every name of the table; both → KEGG_2015; neither → the one reaching more; a tie → KEGG_2015; a name reached
  by neither → that cell not auditable (S undefined); a printed hsa id selects KEGG_2016, v6.3 O4); a family is not
  auditable if the gated table naming the cells is absent or no name reaches the KEGG library chosen by §1.6; source 1 is
  looked for in one run per pathway with one `--name` and the table's other printed names as rival-only `--rival` names (v6.3
  O4, v6.4 N4; a list found is corroborated against the chosen library's pathway, v6.5); **RG-15 / RG-V23** (the second
  dysregulated list): **not auditable (S undefined: source 1 uncorroborated), v6.5** — the list is identified only in the
  supplement, no external definition is pinned and no size is printed, so no list could be corroborated (v6.1 – v6.4: S from
  the supplement, provisionally). RG-03, RG-V06, RG-V07 are S conditional on the gated supplements and counted provisionally
  (§1.8; §4's lower view counts them not auditable). RG-V23's two maps carry
  map index 0 = Val1, 1 = Val2 (§1.9; v6 R7). **RG-V26** (a third validation cohort, identity in the unread supplement):
  not auditable (cohort unidentified).
  Levels: RG-02, RG-03, RG-V03 … V07 gene null only (uniform; Enrichr); RG-04 … RG-15, RG-V08 … V23 gene null only
  (structured: gene-length covariate); RG-V26 other (unstated).
- Within-paper nulls (upregulated, rare de novo, common variants, other cell classes, M9 / M19 / M20): descriptor cells
  RG-N01 … N10 (conditional as the claims; RG-N04 … N07: S conditional on the mixed supplements, v6 R2, corroborated by the
  printed size 500; RG-N03: not auditable (S undefined: source 1 uncorroborated), v6.5); RG-N11 (ADHD
  ΔCT): not auditable (map unpublished).
- Focal (per-row descriptor): RG-06 (the headline finding, a PLS-enrichment form; its status is "not testable as
  stated").

### 2.5 Berto 2022 (berto2022connectivity) — target 5 — gate G1
- **BE-01:** not testable as stated (differential correlation between tissue cohorts). **BE-02 … BE-05:** neurotypical-side
  claims → not ASD claims, and not auditable (controlled expression). **BE-06 … BE-13:** not auditable (their lists need
  controlled PsychENCODE expression; no AHBA re-derivation registered, Q4 option a). Levels: BE-01, BE-04, BE-05 none;
  BE-02, BE-03, BE-06 … BE-13 gene null only (uniform).
- The code-level observation (per-gene statistics across tissue samples paired with random control imaging values;
  `paired.r` with the sample n) is recorded as a note on the paper's inference, from code reading only. PsychENCODE not
  requested. Focal: none eligible.

### 2.6 Rasero 2023 (rasero2023neurogenetics) — target 6 — gate G1
- **Conditional:** the repository notebook `05-mdmr_subtypes_vs_TDC.ipynb` outputs and the journal `mmc1.pdf` are opened
  under G1; auditable iff one holds the subtype-1 and subtype-2 pseudo-R² on the DK regions with labels. Parcels = those
  of the per-gene step as notebook `08-calculate_gen_pvals.ipynb` or the journal Methods state them (34 → dk-ctx; 34 + 7
  → V-DK); undeterminable → not auditable (definition not applicable).
- Claims are defined from preprint v1. **Declared:** unless the version of record is read before the freeze (§6), the
  register audits the preprint's claims, labelled "preprint v1 claims (journal version unread)"; a difference found
  later in a claim-defining element is recorded, never used to redefine a claim.
- **RA-01 … RA-10:** decoded-list enrichment on y_S2 (the paper's per-gene statistic is a spatial-lag t and its tested
  list the signed NEG list; our T is §1.4's mean per-gene Pearson r), direction −; S = the category (WebGestalt GO BP /
  Reactome GMT of the current server, the paper's 2022 release not being obtainable; "S re-derived", "release
  substituted", §1.11 item 2; the category reached by the name rule — RA-01, RA-04, RA-07 by (c), case, the others by
  (a)) **∩ the 2,587 HPA brain-specific genes** (the paper's universe, as Segura's nesting);
  level gene null only (uniform; GSEA). **Focal RA-02** (per-row descriptor): the text's first theme is "glutamate
  signaling (affecting both
  AMPA and NMDA receptors)"; two Fig. 4A categories carry glutamate in their names (RA-02 "Glutamate binding, activation
  of AMPA receptors and synaptic plasticity", RA-04 "Glutamate receptor signaling pathway"); the rule takes the first in
  Fig. 4A order.
- **RA-11 … RA-14:** selected lists, component level; re-selection by the paper's rule on every permutation and surrogate
  with the ML_Lag weights of the paper's code (notebook 08), universe = brain-specific ∩ matrix, an empty re-selected list
  → T = 0; if the weights cannot be read from the code → not auditable (definition not applicable). Level other.
- Within-paper nulls (RA-N1 … N4: the undivided ASD group, the finer subtypes, subtype 1, the TDC split): descriptor
  cells; RA-N1, N2, N4 not auditable (their maps are unpublished); RA-N3 (subtype 1, map y_S1) is conditional on the
  same carrier as RA-11 … 14 and is run as RA-01 … 10's categories on y_S1, two-sided; the Dice robustness figures are
  not registered.

### 2.7 Buch 2023 (buch2023asdsubgroups) — target 7 — gate G1
- **Conditional:** `MOESM3.xlsx` (Supplementary Tables 4–5) opened under G1; auditable iff it holds the four per-subgroup
  maps y with ≥ 230 usable ROIs on each map, an ROI being usable iff it has MNI coordinates and a numeric value on that
  map (no ROI ids needed: the sphere rule of §1.7 uses coordinates; §1.11 item 8); Buch's own gene-set definitions, if

  §1.6's table — a set without a buildable comparator is not auditable), are S
  (source 1), else §1.6
  sources 2–3 — for the GO-slim cells, the PANTHER 16.0 GO-slim term the printed figure label reaches by the name rule (v6,
  R1: 2 labels by (a), "GPCR signaling pathway" by (b), 24 by (c)), whose fallback set is **built before the freeze** from
  PANTHER 16.0's own gene-to-GO-slim annotation as served (`PTHR16.0_human`; v6.2 P3, replacing v6.1 Q7's GO 2021-11-16
  annotation): 27 sets of 17 … 246 columns in V-AAL ("response to toxic substance" and "T cell receptor signaling pathway"
  smallest, "G protein-coupled receptor signaling pathway" largest), none below 5, none above 1,000; overlap with v6.1's
  GO-2021 version by HGNC gene (a descriptor) Jaccard 0.077 … 0.708, median 0.314 (`register_sets/buch_goslim_fallback_*.json`;
  §1.11 item 2). Each Buch cell's source-1 names are its printed Fig. 5c–h label (`carrier_tests.json` "source1_lists" →
  "cells", v6.2 P2); a list headed by a more specific PANTHER name, or "Structural constituent of ribosome", is not the
  shorter-named cell's list (rival names, v6.3 O6).
- Claims (rule G): **BUCH-C1 … C4** (component level; level spatial null only) and **102 enrichment claims** (§1.4 PLS
  enrichment, one-sided in the figure's sign; level gene null only (uniform; fGSEA)): the draft's ASD sets (19), non-
  psychiatric (7), vocal learning (3), ADHD (2), Tourette's (2), synaptic GO cells (32), immune GO cells (32), protein
  translation (5) — 102 enrichment claims, **80 in m** (v6.8, B S1; v6.7: 84; v6.6: 80): the row printed "*Ribosomal structure" reaches no PANTHER term (v6,
  R1) and no size is printed, so **BUCH-E-Trans-StructConstRibosome-S1** (and its descriptor cells S2 … S4) are **not
  auditable (S undefined: source 1 uncorroborated), v6.5** (v6 – v6.4: S conditional on MOESM3, R2, counted in m's upper bound
  only; §4); v5's inferred mapping to "structural constituent of ribosome" is withdrawn. **Since v6.6 (B2)** the claims of
  the sets whose comparator is not buildable pre-freeze are **not auditable (S undefined: comparator not buildable
  pre-freeze)** (§1.6's table; declared, C8) — **21 since v6.8** (as in v6.6): ASD downregulated (asdM12) ×4, the focal
  BUCH-E-ASDdown-S1 among them, ASD upregulated (asdM16) ×4, FMRP-interacting ×4, CNS autoimmune ×4, vocal learning ×3
  (S1, S3, S4), Tourette's ×2 (S1, S4); v6.7 (N1) had built asdM12's comparator from Voineagu 2011 Supplementary Table 14
  (17 then), and v6.8 (B S1; post hoc, declared) moves it to the near-miss view — "asdM12" reaches M12 only through
  Parikshak 2013's name, a reference Buch does not cite for the set (R-d applied uniformly). **The registered secondary view
  (v6.7 (1), §1.6):** ASDdown ×4 (v6.8), ASDup ×4, FMRP ×4 and Tourette's S1 / S4 have a near-miss comparator (Voineagu's
  Table 14 through Parikshak 2013's name, Parikshak 2013's "asdM16", Darnell 2011's 842 FMRP targets, DISEASES "Tourette
  syndrome" after the declared normalisation) and count in the view's m′; CNS autoimmune and vocal learning are not
  auditable in both views.
  **Syn-GPCR-S2** is a descriptor cell: its printed value −1.32 would pass the
  FDR .05 encoding, but the text counts 2 of 11 synaptic sets for subgroup 2 and it is not known that the figure marks
  the cell itself (mismatch recorded). **SZ (S1, S4), MDD (S4), GAD (S4)** are figure-only, not cited by the text for any
  finding → descriptor cells. The 16 stated non-enrichments and every other non-significant cell of Fig. 5c–h:

  antisocial PD, neurotic disorder — as having no defined source; wrong, v6.6 C5: the Methods cite one for each, and each

  antisocial PD and neurotic disorder comparators have < 5 columns, so those 12 cells are "not testable (< 5)"). Component
  comparators: the §4.1 check if the carrier holds the variance explained, else descriptive. The descriptor grid of Fig.
  5h is registered exactly as the (already-seen) figure shows it, re-read before the freeze. ED Figs 8–9: out of scope. Route V-AAL (sphere rule, §1.7). **Focal BUCH-E-ASDdown-S1** (Results
  p. 7–8, the first enrichment cell stated; the component claims BUCH-C1 … C4 precede it but are not eligible) — **not
  auditable in the primary view** (S undefined: comparator not buildable pre-freeze; v6.8 B S1, as in v6.6) and auditable in
  the near-miss view only: there its S is Buch's own list in MOESM3 if corroborated (Jaccard ≥ .80) against Voineagu's
  Supplementary Table 14, else that comparator (v6.7 N1 had made this its primary S; declared on the headline, §0).

### 2.8 Mamat 2025 (mamat2025molecular) — target 8 — gate G1
- **Conditional:** Additional files 1–4 opened under G1; auditable iff they hold both FD t-maps (ABIDE I and ABIDE II,
  Schaefer-400, 17 networks) with the 400 parcels given by name, or by index 1 … 400 only if the carrier itself names the
  17-network order file (§1.11 item 8; v6 R9 — else not auditable (carrier test failed)) (for C1, C2: the ABIDE I map;
  C3: the ABIDE II map). No reconstruction from ABIDE (prereg §5.1).
- **Enumerated claims (24; 22 with a verdict — E-SEA-Pnoc-cortex and E-SEA-cholinergic-striatum are not auditable
  (definition not applicable: v4 pinned a mouse table without an orthology rule; v6 R3); E-URG-BP-1, the focal, is in m
  since v6: the name rule reaches GO:0050804 by (b), §1.11 item 2):** MAMAT-C1 (ABIDE I PLS1), C2 (ABIDE I PLS3:
  `PLSRegression(n_components=3, scale=True)`, the smallest model holding component 3, as the paper states no K), C3
  (ABIDE II PLS1): component level, level other
  (unstated), reproduction descriptive (only P < .05 is published). URG (direction +), each term as the paper prints it
  and mapped by the name rule (§1.1; all by (a) except BP-1 (b) and BP-3 (c)): E-URG-BP-1 modulation of synaptic
  transmission (→ GO:0050804 "modulation of chemical synaptic transmission"), BP-2 signaling, BP-3 nervous system
  processes (→ GO:0050877 "nervous system process"), CC-1 mitochondrial membrane, CC-2 respirasome, MF-1 electron
  transfer activity, MF-2 inorganic molecular entity transmembrane transporter activity. DRG (direction −): E-DRG-BP-1
  chromatin organization, BP-2 regulation of cellular response to stress, CC-1 centrosome, CC-2 Golgi membrane, MF-1
  histone binding, MF-2 molecular adaptor activity. Tissue: E-Tissue-URG and E-Tissue-DRG (HPA TissueEnrich "cerebral
  cortex"; + / −). SEA regions (list unstated → two-sided): E-SEA-cerebellum, -cortex, -thalamus, -hippocampus (the
  human brain-region table read as `pSI.data` human$young.adulthood, the token reading "query tokens ⊆ column tokens":
  S-build readings, v6 R3). SEA cell types (two-sided): E-SEA-Pnoc-cortex, E-SEA-cholinergic-striatum — not auditable
  (definition not applicable, R3). Level gene null only (uniform).
- **Each enrichment claim is one test across the two cohort maps** (the paper's lists are a conjunction over both):
  it survives iff the verdict survives on the ABIDE I map **and** on the ABIDE II map (p = the larger conjunction p);
  both maps are needed. Fig. 4B KEGG terms (their release: KEGG REST as captured 2024-09-16, the obtainable KEGG release
  closest in date to the paper's submission, §1.6 KEGG rule (ii), v6.1 Q2 with v6.7 (2)'s archived releases; v6.1 … v6.6:
  Enrichr KEGG_2026), the "neurodegeneration pathways" sentence (no exact KEGG name; contradictory
  between Results and Discussion) and the CSEA developmental sentences (stage ranges) are descriptor families. Route
  dk-ctx (Schaefer rule). **Focal MAMAT-E-URG-BP-1** (Results, "Gene enrichment analyses", first term stated; C1–C3
  precede it but are not eligible; its status is "conditional (G1)" since v6 — v5 read it "not auditable (S undefined)").

- **Deferred**, as prereg §5.1 lists it ("deferred to A2"; A2 is superseded by A3, PROGRAMME.md, Arcs); its definitions

  (AAL3; component level, T = cumulative R² of the paper's two components; level spatial null only for the model-level
  BrainSMASH check, LV1 p .696); VR-E1 … E4 GSEA (KEGG 2021) circadian entrainment (NES 2.043), glutamatergic synapse
  (2.064), retrograde endocannabinoid signalling (2.005), long-term potentiation (1.865) — S = the Enrichr KEGG_2021_Human
  gene lists (the library the paper names, obtained 2026-09-26: v6 R12; built, not run), each reached by (c) of the name
  rule (case; VR-E3 also UK / US spelling) — PLS enrichment, direction +
  (positive = hyperconnectivity-associated), level gene null only (uniform); VR-N1 "hyper and hypo gene sets differ in
  neurotransmitter composition" (P = .257): a comparison of two lists' composition — not testable as stated. All

### 2.10 Liu 2026 (liu2026spatiotemporal) — target 10 — T3 under gate G2; the rest ungated
- **LIU-T1** (prenatal enrichment), **LIU-T2** (thalamus vs frontal cortex), **LIU-T4** (neocortex), **LIU-T5**
  (hippocampus): no spatial map → "no calibrated test" (amendment 5 §3); focal: none eligible (no full-verdict form); G1–G5 descriptors on the paper's per-sample
  statistic (G5 labelled "matching variable = tested quantity; expected null by construction"). Level none. Pinned: S =
  Satterstrom 2020 (102) ∪ Grove 2019 MAGMA (25) ∪ SFARI score 1 at the release rule of §1.6 (Liu's count 233: v6.7 (2),
  the uniform archive rule — release 03-28-2024 as exported 2024-05-15, score 1 = 233, the earliest release with Liu's
  count; merged S 258 HGNC-mapped, 249 in the class universe; v6 … v6.6: "release substituted", 2026-07-12, score 1 = 245,
  270 / 257; §1.11 item 2); BrainSpan
  RNA-seq genes matrix; prenatal = samples aged in pcw, postnatal = mos / yrs; frontal cortex = BrainSpan structures
  DFC, VFC, MFC, OFC; thalamus = MD, DTH; striatum = STR; cerebellum = CBC; hippocampus = HIP; amygdala = AMY (the SD4
  contrasts of frontal cortex against striatum, cerebellum and hippocampus are registered as descriptor cells); T4 / T5 on AHBA samples assigned to DK labels by abagen ("re-derived": the
  paper's manual AHBA → ENIGMA map is unpublished).
- **LIU-T3:** published-null (P_FDR .057, "trend"); y = ENIGMA meta thickness + subcortical volume d_icv, L/R averaged as
  the paper, on the left V-DK parcels (two classes), set-vs-map, two-sided; §4.1 comparator: Spearman ρ of the paper's
  per-parcel rank-biserial enrichment of S (recomputed on our matrix) with y, against −.34; the Liu-note recomputation is
  declared (§0).
- LIU-T4 / T5 comparators: the claim's statistic computed from our per-label rank-biserial r against the same statistic
  computed from the paper's SD7 values (they cannot survive, so the flag does not move the population, §1.2).
- **LIU-T6:** not auditable (module memberships unpublished; level gene null only (uniform)); gate none. T7 not a claim.

## 3. The added targets (amendment 4 §1; descriptive, never headline)

### 3.1 Pagani 2026 (pagani2026crossspecies) — target 13 — gate G1
- y = the paper-cited GitHub maps @ef58cf1 (hypo, hyper), asserted byte-equal to NeuroVault 14698 images 798768 / 798767
  (on a difference the GitHub files govern); route **V-AAL** re-parcellated (whole-brain voxel maps; NeuroVault brain
  coverage 67.9 %; the decoder mask the paper used is unstated, so the class covering every tissue is used). S = the list
  Supplementary Table 3 ("Brain decoded genes and gene lists used in enrichment analyses in humans") presents as the set's
  input membership (source 1, by the source-1 criterion, v6.1 Q1 as revised by v6.2 P1 / P2 / P6 — its decoded genes, a
  result on the paper's own maps, never qualify; per cell, the set name the paper prints, `carrier_tests.json`
  "source1_lists" → "cells"), else §1.6's sources 2–3 in order. **Declared consequence (v6.2, known before the gate):**
  the table's title as the supplement index prints it holds both input wording ("gene lists used in enrichment analyses")
  and result wording ("decoded genes"); if a sheet carries that title as its caption, every list on it is refused (result
  wording in the caption, §1.6 (c)) and the cells take the else branch. **(v6.3, O2)** Input wording also counts in a list's
  own forward-filled title (plant PAG3), but **since v6.4 (N1) any sheet that also holds a decoded list, or names hypo /
  hyper, refuses every list on it** (PAG1, PAG3 and the v6.2 Pagani layout are refused); only input lists on a sheet of their
  own can qualify, and **since v6.5 only if corroborated** — Jaccard ≥ .80 with the cell's comparator, **built before the
  freeze since v6.6 (B2; §1.6's table)**: Gandal 2022's 24 DE gene modules (C1, C2), Reactome 92 (C3, C5 … C8), SynGO 1.2
  (C4), Gandal's modules "immune response" (GeneM7, C11) and "reactive microglia" (GeneM8, C12); PAG-C9 ("vesicle transport
  and release") and PAG-C10 ("synaptic vesicles and plasticity") reach no module label Gandal prints and are **not auditable
  (S undefined: comparator not buildable pre-freeze)** — in the registered secondary view (v6.7 N6, §1.6) they take Gandal's
  GeneM3 and GeneM5, whose Results text describes them in Pagani's words ("comparator from a near-miss source (not the cited
  reference) / normalised name / name bridge / note reading" — v6.10: their route is a note reading; counted in m′ only; v6.8 C: each module found by a content-word search of Gandal's article
  with a uniqueness assert, not picked by hand). Source 1 is expected to fail
  for Pagani in most layouts — the safe direction: the cells then take §1.6's sources 2–3, never a decoded list. Sign
  composition unstated → two-sided, labelled.
- **PAG-C1 … C12** (decoded-list enrichment; level gene null only (uniform); PAG-C9, C10 not auditable since v6.6, so 10
  in m); PAG-C5 registered as printed (OR .92
  reported as enrichment); the subtype dissociation is not registered. Focal-for-description **PAG-C1**.

### 3.2 Benkarim 2021 (benkarim2021connectivity) — target 14 — gate G1
- y = NeuroVault 8667 images 404555 (`sdistRaw`) and 404554 (`edistRaw`) (§1.7; 404560 excluded as a mislabelled
  entropy file); volumes → **dk-ctx** re-parcellated.
- **BENK-C1:** CSEA sets (pSI < .05) {cortex, cerebellum, striatum} × {adolescence, young adulthood} × {SD, DD} = 12
  claims, **two-sided** (the text fixes no sign; the code's upper-tail selection does not count, §1.4), level gene null
  only (uniform; spin upstream). **BENK-C2:** not testable as stated (genome-wide robust regression of decoding t on
  log2FC); descriptors: the paper's RLM on our decoding (reproduction) and a Gandal 2018 ASD-down set proxy (labelled).
  Focal-for-description BENK-C1 cortex × adolescence × SD.

- Orientation: y is oriented by Supp Table 4's own header or legend; if they do not state it, DEAR-C1 / C2 are
  two-sided.
- y = Supp Table 4 ASD column (the repo CSV compared as a check); components = the authors' DK projection
  `outputs/ahba_dme_scores_in_dk.csv` @ab7939c; the bilateral map on left parcels, as the paper; **dk-ctx**.
- **DEAR-C1** and **DEAR-C2**: component level, T = r(y, C_k), direction per the orientation rule above (+ and − if Supp
  Table 4 states the shrinkage orientation the paper reads, else two-sided), level spatial null only; §4.1 check against
  .49 / −.28 (an opposite sign → "not reproduced (sign)"). DEAR-C3 descriptor (two-sided).

  **PRE-X1:** not testable as stated (an overlap between two decoded lists; no map form) and not auditable. Descriptor

  2 — R2's conditional form examined and not met: FUMA's sets are FUMA's, and Pretzsch publishes no gene names); PRE-D1r (SA-negative × Gandal ASD-up; k 8, OR 0.4, p 1) and PRE-DG-01 … 06 (the other six Gandal rows of eTables
  3–4, §1.11 item 4), two-sided.

### 3.6 Moreau 2020 (moreau2020mutations) — target 18 — ungated (CNV class, reported apart)
- y = the code's nodal profiles (β block, diagonal zeroed, mean over 64); X = the locus genes. **Native MIST64** with a
  §3.1-settings MIST64 matrix (MIST atlas, figshare 10.6084/m9.figshare.5633638), "calibration extrapolated (class)".
  **Primary parcels: left + midline by prereg §2's own rule** (hemisphere from each parcel's centroid, B = |x| < 2 mm),
  and tissue classes (cortex / subcortex / cerebellum; AMY_HIPP as subcortex) fixed before the freeze from the MIST
  atlas and written into the TSV with the resulting counts; all 64 = the bilateral multiverse cell and the reproduction
  setting.
- **MOR-C1** (16p11.2), **MOR-C2** (22q11.2): component level, T = cumulative R² of two components with prereg §4.7's
  `PLSRegression(2, scale=True)`; §4.1 check with the authors' centred, unscaled PLS on all 64 against 24.2 % / 43.2 %.
  Level other (subject-label permutation); the paper's own random-gene null run and failed (p .1782 / .8211; median-r
  .31 / .36). MOR-C3 descriptor; MOR-T1, T2, R1 … R4 not testable as stated; MOR-G excluded; the workbook's ABIDE sheets
  stay unopened.

### 3.7 Hansen 2022 (hansen2022local) — target 19 — gate G2
- y = `enigma_ct.csv` column asd, left rows (Cammoun order 35–68) → **dk-ctx**; component = the shipped `gene_pc1.csv`
  left rows (the paper's object); our §3.1 PC1 (DS > .1) a descriptor.
- **HAN-C1:** component level, T = r(y, gene PC1), two-sided, level none (amendment 4 §1's "1,000 spins" belongs to
  other analyses; recorded). The paper never states this pair's association; it enters only as gene PC1's dominance
  (.027) in the ASD model — the reading says so. Reproduction descriptive (R²_adj .533, dominance .027 with the shipped
  predictors).

### 3.8 Leyhausen 2024 (leyhausen2024intrinsic) — target 20 — gate G2
- y = NeuroVault 12301 images 777428 (MSD), 777429 (PF), 777430 (RF), attributed by §1.7 (v1's "unresolved" becomes
  "attribution: metadata (version inferred)"); uploaded as surfaces → the left fsaverage GIFTIs → **dk-ctx** (surface
  rule). Sign composition unstated → two-sided. Level gene null only (uniform). S pinned by §1.6 (Gandal 2018, Parikshak
  2016, Velmeshev 2019, Kang 2011, Polioudakis 2019; KEGG at Enrichr KEGG_2021_Human, the obtainable release closest in date
  to the paper's retrieval 2021-12-14 — §1.6 KEGG rule (ii), v6.1 Q3, "release substituted"; the Fig. 3B labels,
  transcribed in lower case, reach their pathways by (c) of the name rule, case, except LEY-K4 — each row labelled).
- **LEY-D1 … D9, LEY-K1 … K6, LEY-M1 … M4, LEY-C01 … C13** (32); LEY-M1 … M4 not auditable (S undefined: Kang 2011
  publishes no module membership, §1.11 item 2; R2's conditional form cannot be met — Leyhausen's only gated objects are
  NeuroVault images, and its supplements, read before the register, publish no module list; an exception confirmed by the
  orchestrator, v6.1 Q5); **LEY-K4** not auditable (S undefined: "pathways of neurodegeneration - multiple diseases"
  reaches no KEGG_2021_Human pathway by the name rule — that release names hsa05022 "Pathways of neurodegeneration";
  v6.1 Q3). **Cause (v6.2, P4):** Enrichr's KEGG_2021_Human truncates KEGG names at " - " (none of the 27 KEGG REST names
  holding " - " appears in full; 18 appear only as the part before it), and the pathway was hsa05022 at retrieval (the
  library's entry is closest to hsa05022, symbol Jaccard 0.967; §1.11 item 2). So 27 with a verdict (v6: 28). Focal-for-description LEY-D1. LEY-META not
  auditable (S undefined): the text names three GO terms exactly ("nervous system development", "synaptic signaling",
  "neuron projection morphogenesis"), but as Metascape cluster representatives whose per-map significance is unpublished (the
  figure's colour scale starts at p = 1), so no map-by-set cell can be fixed; level gene null only (uniform; Metascape
  hypergeometric).
