## 4. Counts, the TSV, and the per-row table
`claims_register.tsv` (one row per registered cell: row number, target number, cell id, cell class, map, set, set_id,
set_size_in_class_universe, set_sha256, set_rule_labels, set_status, T form, direction, analysis class, route, inference
level, status, headline population in / out with reason, reproduction comparator, published value and location, gate,
dependencies, cell_index) is compiled from this file, the drafts and the pre-freeze outputs (§1.11) and frozen with it;
its row numbers key the seeds.

**Claims with a verdict (m) and the population (§1.2), as the TSV gives them (families count once).** The fenced block
below governs any of its numbers repeated in the prose of this register (v6.1 Q4). m is a range (v6, R2): its lower bound
counts the claims whose S is unconditional (`verdict_counted_in_m` = yes), its upper bound also the claims whose S is
conditional on a gated carrier (the value "yes (upper bound only …)"). **Since v6.5 the two bounds are equal (230; 207 in
v6.6; 211 in v6.7; 207 since v6.8):** the
only such claim, BUCH-E-Trans-StructConstRibosome-S1, is not auditable (S undefined: source 1 uncorroborated) — so m no longer
assumes a carrier the criterion may refuse (v6.4 N3's caveat on the upper bound of 231 is void). The provisional rows of the
composition (the Zhang claims RG-04, RG-V08, RG-V09 and the RG KEGG families) still depend on their carriers passing the
criterion and corroboration; a structural refusal (§1.6) sends them to "not auditable (S undefined)", so the realised
population leans toward the lower view.
**The registered secondary view (v6.7 (1), §1.6) has its own m′** — "m.secondary.near_miss" below: m plus the claims whose
primary S is undefined only for want of a buildable comparator and which have a built near-miss comparator ("comparator from
a near-miss source (not the cited reference) / normalised name / name bridge / note reading", v6.9 C3, v6.10) with ≥ 5 columns — **m′ = 223 = 207 + 16** (BUCH-E-ASDdown-S1
… S4 — v6.8, B S1: the focal claim of frozen-11 row 6 among them, auditable in this view only, §0 —, BUCH-E-ASDup-S1 … S4,
BUCH-E-FMRP-S1 … S4, BUCH-E-TS-S1, S4, PAG-C9, PAG-C10; v6.7: 211 + 12; v6.9 C5: BUCH-E-RDNV-S1 … S4 join the view as
descriptor cells and are not counted); the TSV marks each in
`secondary_view_near_miss_status`. m′ is reported beside m, never instead of it; no primary status, population count or
composition below depends on the view.
Conditional *carriers* (Rasero, Buch, Mamat, Romero-Garcia's components) are counted in both bounds, as in v5; Dear's two
component claims stay counted while deferred pending provenance; Vairam / Roy's deferred claims cannot run in A1 and are
not counted. The population is stated **at most** (≤, v6 R10: sets built under gates can fall below 5 columns and leave
it); its composition counts each claim once by §1.8's precedence (R8), with the claims whose S is conditional (R2, and
the RG KEGG families — five from v6.1 to v6.4, three since v6.5: RG-03, RG-V06, RG-V07) marked provisional and a "lower view" that counts them as not auditable; the cross-tab of component-level-in-form × not auditable
stands beside it (the component-form claims counted as not auditable are PAT-C4, RG-V24, RG-V25). Interim = every gate
closed; "gate.Gx.units" = the population units listed apart behind each gate; "buch.units" = Buch's share. The exact rows
are the TSV's. **These numbers are parsed by the builder and must equal the TSV (v6, R4); a planted edit of any of them
fails the build (`src_register/plant_register_numbers.py`, §7):**


## 6. Operator decisions and confirmations before the freeze
1. **Headline (§1.2):** amendment 5's claim-level fraction as frozen, interim while any gate is closed; the population
   keeps every claim the apparatus cannot test (not auditable, component-level, no calibrated test, not testable as
   stated) as a non-survival, and excludes only what a frozen text excludes. Confirm the reproduction reading (§1.5).
2. **Not testable as stated (§1.4):** keep these claims as flagged non-survivals (as written), or commission an
   amendment adding an unsigned T and a weighted-score T with their own plants before the audit runs (a plant battery of
   several hours). This decides whether Segura's and Romero-Garcia's principal findings and Giacomel's MDD hit are ever
   tested by the verdict.
3. **NeuroVault attribution (§1.7):** confirm or reject the metadata rule with its version rule, written after the
   candidates' metadata were listed (attributes Pagani, Benkarim, Park, Leyhausen; rejects Seelemeyer; visual route
   unused).
4. **Gates (§0):** G1 now waits for A3's ABIDE II decision after P4's failure (a fallback-route amendment, or closing
   A3's ABIDE II arm); G2 waits for the H2 / K3 decision. Both are A3 decisions, below.
5. **Pre-freeze work (§1.10 (0)):** authorise the S-build downloads of the external sets (§1.6, §5; public tables, no
   map), fetching the MIST atlas for Moreau's classes, and re-reading two already-seen items (Buch Fig. 5h; Segura Table
   S7) for the descriptor grids.
6. **Rasero:** fetch the version of record (CC BY; unreachable from this box) before the freeze, or accept auditing the
   preprint's claims (declared, §2.6).
7. The downloads of §5 under their gates.

**Operator answers (2026-09-26, A1 session 8, asked before any pre-freeze work):** item 1 confirmed as written (headline
and §1.5 reproduction reading); item 2 — **keep** the "not testable as stated" claims as flagged non-survivals (no
unsigned / weighted-T amendment); item 3 confirmed (metadata rule with its version rule); item 4 — A3 decisions taken
(§6b); items 5 and 7 approved (pre-freeze S-build downloads, the MIST atlas, the two re-reads; §5 downloads under their
gates after the freeze); item 6 — audit the Rasero preprint's claims (version of record not fetched; declared, §2.6).

**Before publication (v6.7 (4); a list the audit keeps until the results are published):**
1. **CTD:** notify the Comparative Toxicogenomics Database and describe the use of its data (its files' terms: cite CTD,
   link to its pages, notify CTD, give it periodic access to the publication) before publishing any result that uses Buch's
   schizophrenia comparator (CTD's report of 2024-01-31; §1.6).
2. The other sources' terms as each SOURCE.md records them (SFARI Gene: do not redistribute; PMC author manuscripts and
   Science's supplementary material: publisher copyright, local research use) — cite, do not redistribute.

## 6b. The A3 decisions this register now waits on (recorded in A3's ARC)
- **P4 failed** (ρ_P4 .668 < .70): no ABIDE II unsealing under the frozen prereg. Options: (b-ii) raw ABIDE II BIDS
  through our own pipeline (weeks of CPU; must itself be validated on ABIDE I before use), (b-iii) DCAN derivatives with
  a P4-type validation registered before it runs, or close A3's ABIDE II arm (H5, H1, H3 pair b, H4 stage 2; K4 then
  reads "fired (all tests NOT EVALUATED)").
- **H2 / K3 design:** amend before T1 / T2 are opened, or run as frozen with near-zero power. The design probe (A3,
  f437204; synthetic maps only) recommends class-stratified blocks with the registered arms. Stacking the PLS arm lowers
  power. Two-baseline power at a = .9 rises .015 → .102 (V-DK) and .028 → .297 (V-AAL), and the PLS arm stays the
  binding constraint. The same amendment carries H3's joint site surrogates.
- **Decided 2026-09-26 (operator; A3 ARC session 8):** ABIDE II by option (b-iv), a same-route H5 through the
  registered fMRIPrep route with route-data plants before any unsealing → A3 amendment 2 (frozen 2026-09-26); **G1 opens
  by (i)** when A3's ABIDE II results are committed (step 6''s results: H5R and its descriptors, H1, H3 pair b, H4 stage 2,
  K4), or by (ii) if the arm closes and (b-ii) is recorded as declined (by the operator, or
  under the standing delegation with the notice rule of A3 amendment 2's standing-delegation clause, as frozen: the record
  committed no earlier than a later session, one the operator opens (a new ARC session heading), not a continuation, loop
  or scheduled run of the session that gave the notice), or by (iii) on any other A3 path once A3 records that no further
  ABIDE II analysis will run, under the same notice rule (§0 as revised 2026-09-26 and in v6). H2 / K3 + H3 →
  A3 amendment 1 (recommendation (a)–(g)); **G2 opens** once amendment 1 is frozen and A3's step 3 (v) (the T1 / T2 tests)
  is committed (§0 as revised 2026-09-26).
